# dotnet-core library index

.NET fundamentals and runtime guides. Version 11, from <https://github.com/dotnet/docs> at main, commit 94b7dca39d8dca4374f326176874381661f4dd59.
License: CC-BY-4.0, by Microsoft and the .NET docs contributors; see THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md. The text was converted to plain Markdown and cut into files of 16 KB at most.

This index is long: search it with grep for your words, never read it whole. Then open one file and read only the section you need.

- [dnim layout command](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/dnim-cli-layout.md): dnim-win-[x86|x64|arm64] layout - Creates or updates a cache for offline deployments. (2 KB)
- [dnim query products command](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/dnim-cli-query-products.md): dnim-win-[x86|x64|arm64] query products - Queries release information about specific .NET products. (5 KB)

- [dnim trace command](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/dnim-cli-trace.md): dnim-win-[x86|x64|arm64] trace - Identifies running .NET and .NET Core processes and the runtimes and SDK being used. (3 KB)
- [dnim uninstall command](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/dnim-cli-uninstall.md): dnim-win-[x86|x64|arm64] uninstall - Detects, classifies, and removes .NET installations on a device. (15 KB)

- [dnim-cli-except-support-phase](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/includes/dnim-cli-except-support-phase.md): Exclude installations that match the specified support phase. (1 KB)

- [dnim-cli-log-file](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/includes/dnim-cli-log-file.md): Specify the path of the log file. (1 KB)

- [dnim-cli-output-file](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/includes/dnim-cli-output-file.md): Specify the full path of the file where DNIM writes the command results. (1 KB)

- [dnim-cli-remove-orphaned-installs](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/includes/dnim-cli-remove-orphaned-installs.md): Identify and remove orphaned installations that have dangling dependents. (1 KB)
- [dnim-cli-report-issues](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/includes/dnim-cli-report-issues.md): Return a nonzero exit code when the command succeeds but detects installations that require action. (1 KB)

- [dnim-cli-security](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/includes/dnim-cli-security.md): Only include releases that contain security updates. (1 KB)
- [dnim-cli-support-phase](library/dotnet-core/additional-tools/includes/dnim-cli-support-phase.md): Include only products, releases, or installations that match the specified support phase. (1 KB)

- [Breaking changes in .NET Core 3.0 (part 3 of 14)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/3.0-p3.md): Google started to shut down Google+ Sign-in for apps as early as January 28, 2019. (15 KB)
- [Breaking changes in .NET Core 3.0 (part 4 of 14)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/3.0-p4.md): The ASP.NET Core 3.0 release removed the obsolete MemoryCacheOptions APIs. (13 KB)

- [Breaking changes in .NET Core 3.0 (part 7 of 14)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/3.0-p7.md): In ASP.NET Core 3.0, all "pubternal" types in MVC were updated to either be public in a supported namespace or internal as appropriate. (14 KB)
- [Breaking changes in .NET Core 3.0 (part 8 of 14)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/3.0-p8.md): The HandshakeProtocol.SuccessHandshakeData field was removed and replaced with a helper method that generates a successful handshake response given a specific IHubProtocol. (13 KB)

- [Breaking changes in .NET Core 3.0 (part 12 of 14)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/3.0-p12.md): Starting in .NET Core 3.0, in the default case, MSBuild generates a different manifest file name for resource files. (4 KB)
- [Breaking changes in .NET Core 3.0 (part 13 of 14)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/3.0-p13.md): In .NET Framework, the System.Windows.Forms.Control.DefaultFont property was set to Microsoft Sans Serif 8.25 pt. (14 KB)

- [Compatibility](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/categories.md): A behavioral change represents a change to the behavior of a member. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA1416: Platform compatibility](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca1416-platform-compatibility-analyzer.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA1416 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (6 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA1417: OutAttribute on string parameter for P/Invoke](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca1417-outattributes-on-pinvoke-string-parameters.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA1417 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA1831: Use AsSpan instead of Range-based indexers for string](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca1831-range-based-indexer-on-string.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA1831 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA2013: Do not use ReferenceEquals with value types](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca2013-referenceequals-on-value-types.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA2013 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA2014: Do not use stackalloc in loops](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca2014-stackalloc-in-loops.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA2014 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA2015: Do not define finalizers for types derived from MemoryManager<T>](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca2015-finalizers-for-memorymanager-types.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA2015 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA2200: Rethrow to preserve stack details](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca2200-rethrow-to-preserve-stack-details.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA2200 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CA2247: Argument to TaskCompletionSource constructor should be TaskCreationOptions value](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/code-analysis/5.0/ca2247-ctor-arg-should-be-taskcreationoptions.md): .NET code analyzer rule CA2247 is enabled, by default, starting in .NET 5. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: System.diagnostics entry in app.config](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/configuration/7.0/diagnostics-config-section.md): For applications that have an app.config file, the <configuration><configSections> entry is no longer allowed to contain a entry. (3 KB)
- [Default .NET container tags now use Ubuntu](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/10.0/default-images-use-ubuntu.md): The default Linux distro for .NET tags has been changed from Debian to Ubuntu. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: dotnet test needs a writable VSTest artifacts base directory in containers](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/11/vstest-artifacts-base-directory.md): When you run dotnet test with VSTest in a container, the SDK now needs a writable base directory for private test-session files. (6 KB)
- [.NET 6 breaking change: Default console logger formatting in container images](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/6.0/console-formatter-default.md): The default console formatter that's configured in aspnet containers has changed. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: New non-root 'app' user in Linux images](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/8.0/app-user.md): The .NET Linux container images include a new non-root user named app. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Default ASP.NET Core port changed from 80 to 8080](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/8.0/aspnet-port.md): The default ASP.NET Core port configured in .NET container images has been updated from port 80 to 8080. (6 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'ca-certificates' package removed from Alpine images](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/8.0/ca-certificates-package.md): The ca-certificates package is no longer installed in .NET Alpine container images. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Debian container images upgraded to Debian 12](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/8.0/debian-version.md): The default Linux distro for .NET container images is Debian. (1 KB)
- [Kerberos package removed from Alpine and Debian images](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/8.0/krb5-libs-package.md): Kerberos is no longer installed in .NET Alpine and Debian container images. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'libintl' package removed from Alpine images](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/8.0/libintl-package.md): The libintl package is no longer included in .NET's Alpine container images. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Multi-platform container tags are Linux-only](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/8.0/multi-platform-tags.md): The .NET 8 multi-platform container tags have been updated to be Linux-only. (2 KB)
- [.NET Monitor images simplified to version-only tags](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/9.0/monitor-images.md): The .NET Monitor 9 image offering has been simplified to only provide Azure Linux distroless images. (4 KB)
- [.NET 9 container images no longer install `zlib`](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/containers/9.0/no-zlib.md): .NET 9 container images no longer install zlib since it's not a dependency of the .NET Runtime anymore. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: ActivitySource.CreateActivity and ActivitySource.StartActivity behavior changes](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/activity-sampling.md): The System.Diagnostics.ActivitySource.CreateActivity and System.Diagnostics.ActivitySource.StartActivity APIs only return an System.Diagnostics.Activity when there's a registered listener that decides the instance should be created. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change - System.Linq.AsyncEnumerable in .NET 10](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/asyncenumerable.md): .NET 10 introduces the System.Linq.AsyncEnumerable class, which provides a full set of LINQ extension methods for the System.Collections.Generic.IAsyncEnumerable1 type. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change - BufferedStream.WriteByte no longer performs implicit flush](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/bufferedstream-writebyte-flush.md): The System.IO.BufferedStream.WriteByte(System.Byte) method no longer performs an implicit flush when the internal buffer is full. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: C# 14 overload resolution with span parameters](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/csharp-overload-resolution.md): C# 14, which ships with .NET 10, introduces new built-in span conversions and type inference rules. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Default trace context propagator updated to W3C standard](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/default-trace-context-propagator.md): The default trace context propagator has been switched from the legacy propagator to the W3C propagator. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'DynamicallyAccessedMembers' annotation removed from 'DefaultValueAttribute' ctor](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/defaultvalueattribute-dynamically-accessed-members.md): The System.ComponentModel.DefaultValueAttribute.#ctor(System.Type,System.String) constructor is no longer annotated with System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMembersAttribute. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: DriveInfo.DriveFormat returns Linux filesystem types](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/driveinfo-driveformat-linux.md): On Linux systems, System.IO.DriveInfo.DriveFormat now returns Linux kernel filesystem type strings. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - FilePatternMatch.Stem changed to non-nullable](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/filepatternmatch-stem-nonnullable.md): The Microsoft.Extensions.FileSystemGlobbing.FilePatternMatch.Stem property was previously annotated as nullable because the PatternTestResult.Stem property it gets its value from is nullable. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Consistent shift behavior in generic math](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/generic-math.md): Shift operations in generic math now have consistent behavior across all built-in integer types. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Specifying explicit struct Size disallowed with InlineArray](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/inlinearray-explicit-size-disallowed.md): Applying explicit Size to a struct decorated with System.Runtime.CompilerServices.InlineArrayAttribute is ambiguous and no longer supported in the type loader. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: LDAP DirectoryControl parsing is now more stringent](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/ldap-directorycontrol-parsing.md): Previously, .NET used System.DirectoryServices.Protocols.BerConverter to parse the System.DirectoryServices.Protocols.DirectoryControl objects it received over the network and to generate the System.DirectoryServices.Protocols.DirectoryControl byte arrays it sent. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: MacCatalyst version normalization](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/maccatalyst-version-normalization.md): This update ensures that MacCatalyst version components retrieved from the OS are always normalized to three components: major, minor, and build. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET 10 obsoletions with custom IDs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/obsolete-apis.md): Some APIs have been marked as obsolete, starting in .NET 10. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET runtime no longer provides default termination signal handler](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/sigterm-signal-handler.md): On Unix systems, the .NET runtime no longer provides a default SIGTERM signal handler. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Arm64 SVE nonfaulting loads require mask parameter](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/10.0/sve-nonfaulting-loads-mask-parameter.md): All Arm64 SVE nonfaulting load APIs have been updated to include a mask parameter in the first position. (5 KB)

(2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CborReader and CborWriter enforce a default maximum nesting depth](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/cbor-max-depth.md): Starting in .NET 11, System.Formats.Cbor.CborReader and System.Formats.Cbor.CborWriter enforce a maximum nesting depth by default. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: System.Numerics.Complex special-value results now follow C23 Annex G](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/complex-annex-g-special-values.md): System.Numerics.Complex arithmetic operators and math functions now produce the special-value results (for signed zeros, infinities, and NaNs) required by C23 Annex G (IEC 60559-compatible… (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: DateOnly and TimeOnly TryParse methods throw for invalid input](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/dateonly-timeonly-tryparse-argumentexception.md): The System.DateOnly and System.TimeOnly TryParse and TryParseExact methods now throw an System.ArgumentException when invalid System.Globalization.DateTimeStyles values or format specifiers are provided. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: Decimal and BigInteger floating-point conversions are correctly rounded](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/decimal-biginteger-floating-point-conversions.md): Conversions between System.Decimal and binary floating-point types, and conversions from System.Numerics.BigInteger to binary floating-point types, now produce correctly rounded results. (7 KB)
- [Breaking change: DeflateStream and GZipStream write headers and footers for empty payload](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/deflatestream-gzipstream-empty-payload.md): System.IO.Compression.DeflateStream and System.IO.Compression.GZipStream now always write format headers and footers to the output stream, even when no data is written. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Environment.GetCommandLineArgs()0 preserves the apphost invocation name](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/environment-getcommandlineargs-apphost-invocation-name.md): When you launch an app through its apphost, System.Environment.GetCommandLineArgs now preserves the invocation name, including a relative path or symlink name. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Environment.TickCount made consistent with Windows timeout behavior](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/environment-tickcount-windows-behavior.md): On Windows, System.Environment.TickCount and System.Environment.TickCount64 have been updated to be consistent with the behavior seen in the underlying wait APIs for the OS. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: Math.Round and MathF.Round return correctly rounded results](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/math-round-digits.md): System.Math.Round(System.Double,System.Int32) and System.MathF.Round(System.Single,System.Int32), and their System.MidpointRounding overloads, now return the value that's correctly rounded to the requested number of fractional digits based on the… (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: Named mutexes default to user scope on Unix](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/named-mutexes-unix-default-scope.md): Named mutexes on Unix now default to user scope instead of global scope. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: NamedPipeServerStream with PipeOptions.CurrentUserOnly tightens Unix socket file permissions](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/namedpipeserverstream-unix-permissions.md): To better align on-disk permissions with the documented intent of System.IO.Pipes.PipeOptions.CurrentUserOnly and with the Windows implementation, the underlying Unix domain socket file is now… (6 KB)
- [Breaking change: Nullable.GetUnderlyingType throws for custom Type subclasses](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/nullable-getunderlyingtype-throws.md): System.Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(System.Type) now forwards to the new virtual method System.Type.GetNullableUnderlyingType(). (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET 11 obsoletions with custom IDs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/obsolete-apis.md): Some APIs are marked as obsolete, starting in .NET 11. (3 KB)

- [Breaking change: SafeFileHandle.IsAsync and FileStream.IsAsync accurately reflect non-blocking state on Unix](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/safefilehandle-isasync-unix.md): On Unix, Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles.SafeFileHandle.IsAsync and System.IO.FileStream.IsAsync now accurately reflect whether the underlying file descriptor has the ONONBLOCK flag set. - [Breaking change: TAR-reading APIs verify header checksums when reading](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/tar-checksum-validation.md): The System.Formats.Tar.TarReader class now validates the checksum of TAR archive entries during the reading process. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: TarWriter uses HardLink entries for hard-linked files](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/tarwriter-hardlink-entries.md): System.Formats.Tar.TarWriter now detects when multiple files are hard-linked to the same inode. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change - Tensor operations align equivalent shapes and empty tensors](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/tensor-shape-alignment.md): Starting in .NET 11, tensor operations use consistent shape-alignment rules. (6 KB)
- [Breaking change - Tensor operations reject unsupported storage layouts](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/tensor-storage-layout-validation.md): Starting in .NET 11, multidimensional Tensor copying and destination-taking operations reject unsupported overlapping storage layouts with System.ArgumentException before they write output. (6 KB)
- [Breaking change: Generated WMI wrappers implement IDisposable](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/wmi-wrappers-implement-idisposable.md): Starting in .NET 11, strongly typed WMI collection wrappers and their enumerators generated by ManagementClass.GetStronglyTypedClassCode implement System.IDisposable. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: ZipArchive.CreateAsync eagerly loads ZIP archive entries](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/ziparchive-createasync-eager-load.md): System.IO.Compression.ZipArchive.CreateAsync now eagerly loads all ZIP archive entries during the method call. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: CRC32 validation added when reading ZIP archive entries](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/11/ziparchive-entry-crc32-validation.md): Starting in .NET 11, the System.IO.Compression library validates CRC32 checksums when reading ZIP archive entries. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Assembly-related API behavior changes for single-file publishing format](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/assembly-api-behavior-changes-for-single-file-publish.md): Multiple APIs related to an assembly's file location have behavior changes when they're invoked in a single-file publishing format. - [Breaking change: Most code access security APIs are obsolete](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/code-access-security-apis-obsolete.md): Most code access security (CAS)-related types in .NET are now obsolete as warning. (14 KB)
- [Breaking change: CreateCounterSetInstance throws InvalidOperationException if instance already exists](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/createcountersetinstance-throws-invalidoperation.md): Starting in .NET 5, System.Diagnostics.PerformanceData.CounterSet.CreateCounterSetInstance(System.String) throws an System.InvalidOperationException instead of an System.ArgumentException if the counter set already exists. - [Breaking change: Default ActivityIdFormat is W3C](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/default-activityidformat-changed.md): The default identifier format for activity (System.Diagnostics.Activity.DefaultIdFormat) is now System.Diagnostics.ActivityIdFormat.W3C. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Environment.OSVersion returns the correct operating system version](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/environment-osversion-returns-correct-version.md): System.Environment.OSVersion returns the actual version of the operating system (OS) instead of, for example, the OS that's selected for application compatibility. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: FrameworkDescription's value is .NET instead of .NET Core](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/frameworkdescription-returns-net-not-net-core.md): System.Runtime.InteropServices.RuntimeInformation.FrameworkDescription now returns ".NET" instead of ".NET Core". (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Global assembly cache APIs are obsolete](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/global-assembly-cache-apis-obsolete.md): .NET Core and .NET 5 and later versions eliminate the concept of the global assembly cache (GAC) that was present in .NET Framework. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: Hardware intrinsic IsSupported checks may differ for nested types](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/hardware-instrinsics-issupported-checks.md): Checking <Isa>.X64.IsSupported, where <Isa> refers to the classes in the System.Runtime.Intrinsics.X86 namespace, may now produce a different result to previous versions of .NET. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: IntPtr and UIntPtr implement IFormattable](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/intptr-uintptr-implement-iformattable.md): System.IntPtr and System.UIntPtr now implement System.IFormattable. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: LastIndexOf has improved handling of empty search strings](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/lastindexof-improved-handling-of-empty-values.md): System.String.LastIndexOf and related APIs now return correct values when searching for a zero-length (or zero-length equivalent) substring within a larger string. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: URI paths with non-ASCII characters parse correctly on Unix](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/non-ascii-chars-in-uri-parsed-correctly.md): A bug was fixed in the System.Uri class such that absolute URI paths that contain non-ASCII characters now parse correctly on Unix platforms. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET 5 API obsoletions with non-default diagnostic IDs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/obsolete-apis-with-custom-diagnostics.md): Some APIs have been marked as obsolete, starting in .NET 5. (14 KB)

- [Breaking change: Complexity of LINQ OrderBy.First{OrDefault} increased](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/orderby-firstordefault-complexity-increase.md): The implementation of System.Linq.Enumerable.OrderBy.System.Linq.Enumerable.First1(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable{0},System.Func{0,System.Boolean}) and System.Linq.Enumerable.OrderBy.System.Linq.Enumerable.FirstOrDefault1(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable{0},System.Func{0,System.Boolean}) has changed, resulting in increased complexity for the operation. - [Breaking change: Microsoft.DotNet.PlatformAbstractions package removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/platformabstractions-package-removed.md): No new versions of the Microsoft.DotNet.PlatformAbstractions NuGet package will be produced. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: PrincipalPermissionAttribute is obsolete as error](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/principalpermissionattribute-obsolete.md): The System.Security.Permissions.PrincipalPermissionAttribute constructor is obsolete and produces a compile-time error. (4 KB)

- [Breaking change: Order of tags in Activity.Tags is reversed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/reverse-order-of-tags-in-activity-property.md): System.Diagnostics.Activity.Tags now stores items in the list according to the order they're added. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: SSE and SSE2 CompareGreaterThan methods properly handle NaN inputs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/sse-comparegreaterthan-intrinsics.md): The following System.Runtime.Intrinsics.X86.Sse and System.Runtime.Intrinsics.X86.Sse2 methods have been fixed to properly handle NaN inputs and match the hardware behavior of the equivalent methods in… (6 KB)

- [Breaking change: UTF-7 code paths are obsolete](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/utf-7-code-paths-obsolete.md): The UTF-7 encoding is no longer in wide use among applications, and many specs now forbid its use in interchange. (8 KB)

- [Breaking change: Vector<T> throws NotSupportedException](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/5.0/vectort-throws-notsupportedexception.md): System.Numerics.Vector1 now always throws a System.NotSupportedException for unsupported type parameters. (2 KB)
- [.NET 6 breaking change: Additional overloads of LINQ Queryable methods](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/additional-linq-queryable-method-overloads.md): Additional public method overloads have been added to System.Linq.Queryable as part of the new features implemented in <https://github.com/dotnet/runtime/pull/47231>. - [Breaking change: Conditional string evaluation in Debug methods](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/debug-assert-conditional-evaluation.md): It's common to use interpolated strings as assert messages, for example: (3 KB)
- [.NET 6 breaking change: New diagnostic IDs for obsoleted APIs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/diagnostic-id-change-for-obsoletions.md): Previously, a few APIs were obsoleted without using custom diagnostic IDs. (3 KB)
- [.NET 6 breaking change: Environment.ProcessorCount behavior on Windows](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/environment-processorcount-on-windows.md): On Windows, the System.Environment.ProcessorCount property now respects process affinity and the job object's hard limit on CPU utilization. - [.NET 6 breaking change: EventSource callback behavior](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/eventsource-callback.md): For an System.Diagnostics.Tracing.EventCommand.Disable, the System.Diagnostics.Tracing.EventSource is now marked as disabled before the callback is issued. (2 KB)
- [.NET 6 breaking change: File.Replace exceptions on Unix](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/file-replace-exceptions-on-unix.md): The behavior of System.IO.File.Replace on Unix-based operating systems has changed. (2 KB)
- [.NET 6 breaking change: FileStream doesn't synchronize file offset with OS](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/filestream-doesnt-sync-offset-with-os.md): To improve performance, System.IO.FileStream no longer synchronizes the file offset with the operating system. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: FileStream locks files with shared lock on Unix](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/filestream-file-locks-unix.md): On Unix, if you open a file using System.IO.FileStream with System.IO.FileAccess.Read permissions only, and then call System.IO.FileStream.Lock(System.Int64,System.Int64) to lock a region of the file,… (3 KB)

- [.NET 6 breaking change: Exception handling in hosting](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/hosting-exception-handling.md): In previous versions, when a Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting.BackgroundService throws an unhandled exception, the exception is lost and the service appears unresponsive. - [Breaking change: Nullable reference type annotation changes](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/nullable-ref-type-annotation-changes.md): In .NET 6, some nullability annotations in the .NET libraries have changed. (8 KB)
- [Breaking change: Standard numeric format parsing precision](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/numeric-format-parsing-handles-higher-precision.md): .NET now supports greater precision values when formatting numbers as strings using ToString and TryFormat. (7 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET 6 obsoletions with non-default diagnostic IDs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/obsolete-apis-with-custom-diagnostics.md): Some APIs have been marked as obsolete, starting in .NET 6. (10 KB)

- [Breaking change: Parameters renamed in Stream-derived types](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/parameters-renamed-on-stream-derived-types.md): In .NET 6, some parameters of methods on types derived from System.IO.Stream have been renamed to match the base class. (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: Partial and zero-byte reads in DeflateStream, GZipStream, and CryptoStream](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/partial-byte-reads-in-streams.md): The Read() and ReadAsync() methods on System.IO.Compression.DeflateStream, System.IO.Compression.GZipStream, and System.Security.Cryptography.CryptoStream might no longer return as many bytes as were requested. - [Breaking change: Set timestamp on read-only file on Windows](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/set-timestamp-readonly-file.md): Setting the timestamp on a file with the read-only attribute now succeeds on Windows and no longer throws an exception. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Static abstract members in interfaces](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/static-abstract-interface-methods.md): .NET 6 previews a new feature where static interface members can be marked as abstract. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: New StringBuilder.Append overloads](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/stringbuilder-append-evaluation-order.md): C# 10 adds support for better string interpolation, including the ability to target custom "handlers" in addition to strings. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Strong-name APIs throw PlatformNotSupportedException](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/strong-name-signing-exceptions.md): A few APIs that aren't supported in .NET/.NET Core but didn't do anything when accessed have been changed to now throw a System.PlatformNotSupportedException at… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: System.Drawing.Common only supported on Windows](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/system-drawing-common-windows-only.md): The System.Drawing.Common NuGet package is now attributed as a Windows-specific library. (8 KB)
- [Breaking change: Task.FromResult may return singleton](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/task-fromresult-returns-singleton.md): System.Threading.Tasks.Task.FromResult1(0) may now return a cached System.Threading.Tasks.Task1 instance rather than always creating a new instance. - [Breaking change: XNodeReader.GetAttribute and invalid indices](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/6.0/xnodereader-getattribute.md): XNodeReader is an internal class, but it's accessible through the System.Xml.XmlReader class if you call System.Xml.Linq.XNode.CreateReader. (2 KB)

- [.NET 7 breaking change: BrotliStream no longer allows undefined CompressionLevel values](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/brotlistream-ctor.md): The System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream constructors that take a System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel argument no longer allow values that aren't defined in the System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel enumeration. - [.NET 7 breaking change: Collectible Assembly in non-collectible AssemblyLoadContext](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/collectible-assemblies.md): .NET incorrectly allowed garbage-collectible assemblies to resolve into a non-collectible System.Runtime.Loader.AssemblyLoadContext. - [.NET 7 breaking change: Validate CompressionLevel for BrotliStream](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/compressionlevel-validation.md): The System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel argument that's passed to System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream constructors is now validated to be one of the defined values of the enumeration. (2 KB)
- [.NET 7 breaking change: Compiling C++/CLI projects in Visual Studio](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/cpluspluscli-compiler-version.md): .NET 7 includes generic math APIs that use static abstract interface members on primitive types, such as System.Int32. (3 KB)
- [.NET 7 breaking change: DateTime addition methods precision change](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/datetime-add-precision.md): In .NET 6 and earlier versions, the value parameter of the DateTime addition methods was rounded to the nearest millisecond. (2 KB)
- [.NET 7 breaking change: Equals method behavior change for NaN](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/equals-nan.md): The Equals(T other) instance method for the following types was updated to meet the System.IEquatable1 implementation requirements. (4 KB)
- [.NET 7 breaking change: Legacy FileStream strategy removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/filestream-compat-switch.md): The AppContext switch System.IO.UseNet5CompatFileStream and the ability to use the legacy System.IO.FileStream implementation were removed. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Maximum precision for numeric format strings](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/max-precision-numeric-format-strings.md): The maximum precision when formatting numbers as strings using ToString and TryFormat has been changed from System.Int32.MaxValue to 999,999,999. - [Breaking change: Tracking linked cache entries](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/memorycache-tracking.md): System.Runtime.Caching.MemoryCache tracks linked cache entries so that options are propagated. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET 7 obsoletions with non-default diagnostic IDs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/obsolete-apis-with-custom-diagnostics.md): Some APIs have been marked as obsolete, starting in .NET 7. (7 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET 7 obsoletions with default diagnostic ID](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/core-libraries/7.0/obsolete-apis-with-default-diagnostic.md): Several APIs have been marked as obsolete in .NET 7. (3 KB)

- [Breaking change: Message no longer duplicated in Console log output](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/extensions/10.0/console-json-logging-duplicate-messages.md): When logging to the console using the JSON formatter, log messages are no longer duplicated in the log output. (3 KB)

- [Breaking change: AddProvider validates provider isn't null](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/extensions/6.0/addprovider-null-check.md): Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.LoggerFactory implements Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.ILoggerFactory with an AddProvider(ILoggerProvider) method. (2 KB)

(2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Binding config to dictionary extends values](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/extensions/7.0/config-bind-dictionary.md): When binding a configuration using a System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary2 object where the value is a mutable collection type, binding to the same key more than once… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: ContentRootPath for apps launched by Windows Shell](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/extensions/7.0/contentrootpath-hosted-app.md): The Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting.IHostEnvironment.ContentRootPath property represents the default directory where appsettings.json and other content files are loaded in a hosted application, including ASP.NET apps. - [Breaking change: Environment variable prefixes](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/extensions/7.0/environment-variable-prefix.md): Hierarchical data is represented using : as the level delimiter. (3 KB)

- [Breaking change: ActivatorUtilities.CreateInstance requires non-null provider](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/extensions/8.0/activatorutilities-createinstance-null-provider.md): The two Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection.ActivatorUtilities.CreateInstance methods now throw an System.ArgumentNullException exception if the provider parameter is null. - [Breaking change: ConfigurationBinder silently skips invalid array elements](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/extensions/8.0/configurationbinder-skips-failed-array-elements.md): Starting in .NET 8, Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration.ConfigurationBinder silently skips array and list elements whose values can't be converted to the target type. - [Breaking change - Casting IDispatchEx COM object to IReflect fails](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/10.0/idispatchex-ireflect-cast.md): Since .NET 5, it was possible to cast a COM object that implements IDispatchEx to System.Reflection.IReflect. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Single-file apps no longer look for native libraries in executable directory](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/10.0/native-library-search.md): Previously, in single-file .NET applications, the directory of the single-file executable was added to the NATIVEDLLSEARCHDIRECTORIES property during startup. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change - Specifying DllImportSearchPath.AssemblyDirectory only searches the assembly directory](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/10.0/search-assembly-directory.md): Starting in .NET 10, if you specify System.Runtime.InteropServices.DllImportSearchPath.AssemblyDirectory as the only search flag, the runtime searches exclusively in the assembly directory. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: NativeAOT uses lib prefix for native library outputs on Unix](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/11/nativeaot-lib-prefix.md): Starting in .NET 11, NativeAOT applies the lib prefix (for example, libmylib.so, libmylib.dylib, libmylib.a) by default to non-executable native library outputs on Unix platforms. - [Breaking change: Native AOT rejects non-blittable UnmanagedCallersOnly signatures](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/11/unmanagedcallersonly-non-blittable.md): In .NET 11, Native AOT enforces the requirement that methods annotated with System.Runtime.InteropServices.UnmanagedCallersOnlyAttribute use only blittable parameter and return types. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: Built-in support for WinRT is removed from .NET](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/5.0/built-in-support-for-winrt-removed.md): Built-in support for consumption of Windows runtime (WinRT) APIs in .NET is removed. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Casting RCW to InterfaceIsIInspectable throws exception](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/5.0/casting-rcw-to-inspectable-interface-throws-exception.md): Casting a runtime callable wrapper (RCW) to an interface marked as System.Runtime.InteropServices.ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIInspectable now throws a System.PlatformNotSupportedException. - [Breaking change: No A/W suffix probing on non-Windows platforms](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/5.0/function-suffix-pinvoke.md): The .NET runtimes no longer add an A or W suffix to function export names during probing for P/Invokes on non-Windows platforms. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: RuntimeInformation.OSArchitecture under emulation](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/7.0/osarchitecture-emulation.md): System.Runtime.InteropServices.RuntimeInformation.OSArchitecture now returns the correct value under emulation. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: CreateObjectFlags.Unwrap only unwraps on target instance](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/8.0/comwrappers-unwrap.md): Previously, if you called System.Runtime.InteropServices.ComWrappers.GetOrCreateObjectForComInstance(System.IntPtr,System.Runtime.InteropServices.CreateObjectFlags) on a System.Runtime.InteropServices.ComWrappers instance with the System.Runtime.InteropServices.CreateObjectFlags.Unwrap flag, a managed object wrapper was unwrapped from any System.Runtime.InteropServices.ComWrappers instance. - [Breaking change: IDispatchImplAttribute API is removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/8.0/idispatchimplattribute-removed.md): The System.Runtime.InteropServices.IDispatchImplAttribute implementation has officially been removed from .NET. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: JSFunctionBinding implicit public default constructor removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/8.0/jsfunctionbinding-constructor.md): The implicit public default constructor for System.Runtime.InteropServices.JavaScript.JSFunctionBinding has been removed. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change - Linux native library resolution no longer uses `netcoredeps`](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/8.0/linux-netcoredeps.md): Starting in .NET 8, Linux applications no longer search the netcoredeps subdirectory for native libraries. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Custom marshallers require additional members](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/8.0/marshal-modes.md): The custom marshaller analyzer has changed to require all element-focused marshal modes to satisfy both the managed-to-unmanaged and unmanaged-to-managed shapes. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: SafeHandle types must have public constructor](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/8.0/safehandle-constructor.md): Historically, passing System.Runtime.InteropServices.SafeHandle-derived types to P/Invokes and COM methods has implicitly required a parameterless constructor of any visibility when a SafeHandle-derived type is passed… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: CET supported by default](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/interop/9.0/cet-support.md): apphost and singlefilehost are now marked as Intel CET—compatible (they're compiled with the /CETCOMPAT option). (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Floating-point conversions to small integral types are saturating](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/jit/11/fp-to-small-integer.md): In .NET 11, unchecked floating-point conversions to sbyte, byte, short, ushort, and char now have saturating behavior at the destination type's bounds. - [Breaking change: Minimum hardware requirements updated](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/jit/11/minimum-hardware-requirements.md): The minimum hardware requirements for .NET 11 have been updated to require more modern instruction sets on x86/x64 architectures. (6 KB)
- [Breaking change: Coerce call arguments according to ECMA-335](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/jit/6.0/coerce-call-arguments-ecma-335.md): ECMA-335 (Table III.9: Signature Matching) describes which implicit conversions are supported for call arguments. (1 KB)
- [Floating point-to-integer conversions are saturating](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/jit/9.0/fp-to-integer.md): Floating point-to-integer conversions now have saturating behavior on x86 and x64 machines. (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: Some SVE APIs removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/jit/9.0/sve-apis.md): Several APIs that take a 32-bit address as an input parameter have been removed because of lack of testing with such addresses. (3 KB)
- [.NET API changes that affect compatibility (part 1 of 3)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/library-change-rules.md): Throughout its history, .NET has attempted to maintain a high level of compatibility from version to version and across implementations of .NET. (3 KB)
- [.NET API changes that affect compatibility (part 2 of 3)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/library-change-rules-p2.md): Changes in this category modify the public surface area of a type. (14 KB)
- [.NET API changes that affect compatibility (part 3 of 3)](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/library-change-rules-p3.md): For example, a method that returns a type of System.Object can return a System.String instance. (10 KB)
- [Breaking change: Minimum Android API level raised to 24](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/android-minimum-api-level.md): The minimum supported Android API level for .NET 11 has been raised from 21 (Android 5.0) to 24 (Android 7.0). (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Android x86 is not supported](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/android-x86-unsupported.md): In .NET 11, Android x86 is not supported. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change - Code generation on mobile changes from Mono to CoreCLR](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/mobile-code-generation.md): .NET 11 mobile apps can use the CoreCLR JIT, ReadyToRun (R2R), and the CoreCLR interpreter. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change - Crash-reporting behavior changes on mobile platforms](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/mobile-crash-reporting.md): In .NET 11, CoreCLR and Mono use different crash handlers and report formats. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Debugging interfaces on mobile platforms changing to ICorDebug](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/mobile-debugger-interfaces.md): In .NET 11, debugging interfaces on mobile platforms change to ICorDebug. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - CoreCLR replaces Mono runtime for .NET mobile apps](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/mobile-runtime-replacement.md): .NET 11 mobile workloads use CoreCLR as their supported runtime. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Mono embedding APIs no longer supported on mobile platforms](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/mono-embedding-apis.md): In .NET 11, Mono embedding APIs are no longer supported on mobile platforms. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Mono project properties no longer configure mobile apps](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/mono-project-properties.md): In .NET 11, Mono project properties no longer configure mobile apps. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Mono runtime settings no longer configure mobile apps](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/mono-runtime-settings.md): In .NET 11, Mono runtime settings no longer configure mobile apps. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - CoreCLR trace events replace Mono trace events](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/runtime-trace-events.md): In .NET 11, CoreCLR trace events replace Mono trace events. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - UnwindNativeCode isn't supported on Apple targets](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/11/unwind-native-code-apple.md): In .NET 11, UnwindNativeCode isn't supported on Apple targets. (1 KB)

- [Breaking change: Optional parameter on UpdateBackground](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/maui/7.0/updatebackground-parameter.md): When updating the button background on iOS, the border was also required to be drawn. (1 KB)

- [Breaking change - MailAddress enforces validation for consecutive dots](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/10.0/mailaddress-consecutive-dots.md): Starting in .NET 10, the System.Net.Mail.MailAddress class enforces stricter validation of email addresses. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: URI length limits removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/10.0/uri-length-limits-removed.md): Methods that create System.Uri instances (constructors and System.Uri.TryCreate factory methods) have historically limited the length of the URI string to around 65,000 characters (exact… (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: HttpClient high-cardinality metrics use observable counters](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/11/http-metrics-observable-counters.md): Starting in .NET 11, the http.client.openconnections and http.client.activerequests metrics use System.Diagnostics.Metrics.ObservableUpDownCounter1 instead of System.Diagnostics.Metrics.UpDownCounter1. - [Breaking change: SslStream server-side AIA certificate downloads disabled by default](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/11/sslstream-aia-downloads-disabled.md): Starting in .NET 11, System.Net.Security.SslStream doesn't download missing intermediate certificates using the Authority Information Access (AIA) extension by default when validating client certificates as… (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: Cookie path handling now conforms to RFC 6265](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/5.0/cookie-path-conforms-to-rfc6265.md): Path-handling algorithms defined in RFC 6265 were implemented for the System.Net.Cookie and System.Net.CookieContainer classes. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Socket.LocalEndPoint is updated after calling SendToAsync](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/5.0/localendpoint-updated-on-sendtoasync.md): System.Net.Sockets.Socket.SendToAsync(System.Net.Sockets.SocketAsyncEventArgs) now updates the value of the System.Net.Sockets.Socket.LocalEndPoint property to the socket's local address. - [Breaking change: MulticastOption.Group doesn't accept a null value](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/5.0/multicastoption-group-doesnt-accept-null.md): System.Net.Sockets.MulticastOption.Group no longer accepts a value of null. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: NegotiateStream and SslStream allow successive Begin operations](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/5.0/negotiatestream-sslstream-dont-fail-on-successive-begin-calls.md): Error cases on security streams are handled differently, and successive calls to BeginAuthenticateAsServer or BeginAuthenticateAsClient may no longer fail. - [Breaking change: WinHttpHandler removed from .NET runtime](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/5.0/winhttphandler-removed-from-runtime.md): The WinHttpHandler class was removed from the System.Net.Http.dll assembly. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Port removed from SPN for Kerberos and Negotiate](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/6.0/httpclient-port-lookup.md): When using System.Net.Http.HttpClient with Kerberos or Negotiate authentication, non-default ports are no longer included in service principal names (SPN) to look up services. - [Breaking change: WebRequest, WebClient, and ServicePoint are obsolete](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/6.0/webrequest-deprecated.md): System.Net.WebRequest, System.Net.WebClient, and System.Net.ServicePoint classes are marked as obsolete and generate a SYSLIB0014 warning at compile time. - [Breaking change: AllowRenegotiation default is false](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/7.0/allowrenegotiation-default.md): The default value of System.Net.Security.SslServerAuthenticationOptions.AllowRenegotiation has been changed to false. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Custom ping payloads on Linux](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/7.0/ping-custom-payload-linux.md): On Linux, non-privileged processes can't send raw IP packets. (3 KB)

- [Breaking change: SendFile throws NotSupportedException for connectionless sockets](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/8.0/sendfile-connectionless.md): The behavior of the System.Net.Sockets.Socket.SendFile method family for connectionless (for example, UDP) sockets is now consistent across all platforms. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: User info in `mailto:` URIs is compared](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/8.0/uri-comparison.md): Previously, System.Uri didn't compare user info when comparing two Uri instances for equality. (2 KB)
- [HttpClientFactory uses SocketsHttpHandler as primary handler](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/9.0/default-handler.md): HttpClientFactory allows you to configure an System.Net.Http.HttpMessageHandler pipeline for named and typed System.Net.Http.HttpClient objects. (6 KB)
- [URI query redaction in HttpClient EventSource events](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/9.0/query-redaction-events.md): In .NET 9, the default behavior of System.Diagnostics.Tracing.EventSource events emitted by System.Net.Http.HttpClient and System.Net.Http.SocketsHttpHandler (EventSource name: System.Net.Http) has been modified to scrub the query,… (3 KB)
- [URI query redaction in IHttpClientFactory logs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/9.0/query-redaction-logs.md): In .NET 9, the default implementation of System.Net.Http.IHttpClientFactory has been modified to scrub query strings when logging URI information. (3 KB)
- [HttpClientFactory logging redacts header values by default](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/9.0/redact-headers.md): HttpClientFactory's default logging includes Trace-level logs that output all the request and response headers. (5 KB)
- [HttpClient metrics report `server.port` unconditionally](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/9.0/server-port-attribute.md): When HttpClient metrics were added in .NET 8, server.port was introduced as a Conditionally Required attribute in accordance with the state of the standard… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: HttpListenerRequest.UserAgent is nullable](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/networking/9.0/useragent-nullable.md): The System.Net.HttpListenerRequest.UserAgent property was previously annotated as being non-nullable, but it was actually nullable in practice. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: More restricted annotations on InvokeMember/FindMembers/DeclaredMembers](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/reflection/10/ireflect-damt-annotations.md): Starting in .NET 10, the System.Reflection APIs System.Reflection.IReflect.InvokeMember, System.Type.FindMembers, and System.Reflection.TypeInfo.DeclaredMembers use more restricted annotations instead of System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMemberTypes.All. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: Type.MakeGenericSignatureType argument validation](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/reflection/10/makegeneric-signaturetype-validation.md): Starting in .NET 10, the System.Type.MakeGenericSignatureType(System.Type,System.Type[]) API validates that the genericTypeDefinition argument is a generic type definition. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: IntPtr no longer used for function pointer types](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/reflection/8.0/function-pointer-reflection.md): As a new reflection feature, a function pointer type is now a System.Type instance with new capabilities such as System.Type.IsFunctionPointer. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Code coverage EnableDynamicNativeInstrumentation defaults to false](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/code-coverage-dynamic-native-instrumentation.md): Running dotnet test --collect:"Code Coverage" now disables dynamic native instrumentation by default. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change - MSBUILDCUSTOMBUILDEVENTWARNING escape hatch removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/custom-build-event-warning.md): The MSBUILDCUSTOMBUILDEVENTWARNING environment variable, which previously allowed custom build events derived from Microsoft.Build.Framework.BuildEventArgs, is no longer supported. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Change default workload configuration from 'loose manifests' to 'workload sets' mode](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/default-workload-config.md): In .NET 10 Preview 2, the default workload update mode changed from "manifests" to "workload-set". (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: `DefineConstants` for target frameworks not available at evaluation time](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/defineconstants-not-available-at-evaluation.md): The target-framework-related DefineConstants items (such as NET, NET90ORGREATER, NETSTANDARD20, and similar constants) are no longer computed at MSBuild evaluation time. - [Breaking change: NuGet packages with no runtime assets aren't included in deps.json](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/deps-json-trimmed-packages.md): NuGet packages or other libraries that don't contribute any runtime assets are now excluded from the deps.json file if removing them wouldn't cause dependency… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - dnx.ps1 file is no longer included in .NET SDK](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dnx-ps1-removed.md): The dnx.ps1 shim script is no longer included in the .NET SDK. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - .NET CLI `--interactive` defaults to `true` in user scenarios](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-cli-interactive.md): The --interactive flag for the .NET CLI now defaults to true in user-centric scenarios. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - dotnet CLI commands log non-command-relevant data to stderr](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-cli-stderr-output.md): Starting in .NET 10, some dotnet CLI command output that isn't core to the command being invoked emits to stderr instead of stdout. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - `dotnet new sln` defaults to SLNX file format](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-new-sln-slnx-default.md): In .NET 10, dotnet new sln generates an SLNX-format solution file instead of an SLN-formatted solution file. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - SHA-1 fingerprint support deprecated in dotnet nuget sign](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-nuget-sign-sha1-deprecated.md): Starting in .NET 10, the NU3043 warning is promoted to an error when using SHA-1 fingerprints with the dotnet nuget sign command. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - dotnet package list command now performs restore by default](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-package-list-restore.md): The dotnet package list command now automatically performs a restore operation before listing packages to ensure accurate and up-to-date results. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - project.json is no longer supported in dotnet restore](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-restore-project-json-unsupported.md): Starting with .NET 10, the dotnet restore command no longer supports project.json based projects. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'dotnet tool install --local' creates manifest by default](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-tool-install-local-manifest.md): When running dotnet tool install --local, a manifest is now created if none exists instead of failing with an error. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET tool packaging might create RuntimeIdentifier-specific tool packages](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-tool-pack-publish.md): The .NET tool packaging process has changed when RuntimeIdentifiers are present in the project. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'dotnet watch' logs to stderr instead of stdout](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/dotnet-watch-stderr.md): Starting in .NET 10, dotnet watch emits its internal-facing log messages to the stderr channel instead of stdout. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - Double quotes in file-level directives are disallowed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/file-level-directive-double-quotes.md): Usage of double quotes " inside #: file-level directives is now a build-time error when running file-based apps (for example, dotnet run app.cs). - [Breaking change - HTTP warnings promoted to errors in dotnet package list and dotnet package search](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/http-warnings-to-errors.md): Starting in .NET 10 Preview 4, HTTP warnings generated by dotnet package list, dotnet package search, and related APIs are now treated as errors… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change - MSBuild custom culture resource handling](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/msbuild-custom-culture.md): Starting in MSBuild 17.14, custom culture resource handling is no longer enabled by default. (2 KB)

- [Breaking change: NUGET_ENABLE_ENHANCED_HTTP_RETRY environment variable removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/nuget-enhanced-http-retry-removed.md): The NUGETENABLEENHANCEDHTTPRETRY environment variable no longer has any effect in .NET 10. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: NuGet logs an error for invalid package IDs](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/nuget-packageid-validation.md): NuGet now validates package IDs when they're used to create URLs in .NET 10. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'dotnet restore' audits transitive packages](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/nugetaudit-transitive-packages.md): The dotnet restore command, which restores the dependencies of a project, now produces security vulnerability warnings for transitive packages by default when the project… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change - PrunePackageReference privatizes direct prunable references](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/prune-packagereference-privateassets.md): The PrunePackageReference feature automatically removes transitive packages that are provided by the target platform. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change - ToolCommandName is not set for non tool packages](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/10.0/toolcommandname-not-set.md): The ToolCommandName property is no longer set automatically for all projects during build or package operations. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Full build skipped for Native AOT publish](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/aot-publish-skips-full-build.md): Starting in .NET 11, the .NET SDK no longer executes the full Build target when publishing with Native AOT (PublishAot=true). (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: dnx scripts bypass global.json SDK selection](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/dnx-scripts-bypass-global-json.md): The dnx and dnx.cmd scripts no longer use the .NET muxer to select the SDK version. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: mono launch target not set for .NET Framework apps](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/mono-launch-target-removed.md): The .NET SDK no longer automatically sets mono as the launch target for .NET Framework applications on Linux when using dotnet run. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: NativeAOT CLI command handling enabled by default](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/native-cli-command-handling-enabled.md): Starting in .NET 11, the .NET SDK CLI enables its NativeAOT-compiled command-handling fast path by default on all platforms. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: SDK local container runtime selection prefers platform-native tools](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/native-local-container-runtimes.md): Starting in .NET 11, when you publish an SDK container to a local container runtime, the SDK automatically prefers platform-native CLIs when they're available:… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: NU1703 warning for packages that use deprecated MonoAndroid framework assets](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/nu1703-deprecated-monoandroid-framework.md): NuGet restore now emits NU1703 when an Android project targets .NET 11 or later and resolves package assets from the deprecated MonoAndroid framework folder. - [Breaking change: NuGet locks directory moved on Linux](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/nuget-locks-directory-linux.md): Starting in .NET SDK 11.0.200, NuGet uses ~/.nuget/locks instead of /tmp/NuGetScratch{username}/locks for inter-process locks on Linux. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: NuGet pack warns for package IDs with restricted characters](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/nuget-pack-nu5052-packageid.md): Starting in .NET 11, dotnet pack emits warning NU5052 when a package ID contains characters outside the restricted character set enforced by nuget.org. - [Breaking change: Restore doesn't search the global packages folder for a higher package version](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/restore-global-packages-folder-version-search.md): When a PackageReference or package dependency requests a package version that doesn't exist, NuGet restore searches for the lowest version that's higher than the… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Template engine packages no longer support netstandard2.0](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/template-engine-netstandard.md): The .NET SDK template engine NuGet packages no longer target netstandard2.0. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Template package installation verifies NuGet signatures](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/template-package-signature-verification.md): Template package installation now verifies signed packages downloaded from NuGet sources when signature verification is enabled. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: VSTest removes dependency on Newtonsoft.Json](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/11/vstest-removes-newtonsoft-json.md): The VSTest platform and Microsoft.NET.Test.SDK NuGet package no longer depend on Newtonsoft.Json. (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: OutputType set to WinExe for WPF and WinForms apps](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/automatically-infer-winexe-output-type.md): OutputType is automatically set to WinExe for Windows Presentation Foundation (WPF) and Windows Forms apps. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Directory.Packages.props files is imported by default](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/directory-packages-props-imported-by-default.md): NuGet's .props files automatically import a file named Directory.Packages.props if it's found in the project folder or any of its ancestors. - [Breaking change: NETCOREAPP3_1 preprocessor symbol is not defined when targeting .NET 5](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/netcoreapp3_1-preprocessor-symbol-not-defined.md): In .NET 5 RC2 and later versions, projects no longer define preprocessor symbols for earlier versions, but only for the version that they target. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Use WindowsSdkPackageVersion to override Windows SDK version](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/override-windows-sdk-package-version.md): Starting in .NET 5.0.8 (which includes .NET SDK 5.0.302 and .NET SDK 5.0.205), developers targeting Windows can't use the FrameworkReference item to override their… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: PublishDepsFilePath behavior change](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/publishdepsfilepath-behavior-change.md): The PublishDepsFilePath MSBuild property is empty for single-file applications. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Error generated when executable references executable](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/referencing-executable-generates-error.md): Generally, an executable project references library projects, not other executable projects. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: WinForms and WPF apps use Microsoft.NET.Sdk](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/sdk-and-target-framework-change.md): Windows Forms and Windows Presentation Framework (WPF) apps now use the .NET SDK (Microsoft.NET.Sdk) instead of the .NET Core WinForms and WPF SDK (Microsoft.NET.Sdk.WindowsDesktop). (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: TargetFramework change from netcoreapp to net](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/5.0/targetframework-name-change.md): The value for the MSBuild TargetFramework property changed from netcoreapp3.1 to net5.0. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Generate apphost for macOS](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/apphost-generated-for-macos.md): When building on macOS, the .NET SDK now produces an executable for app projects. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: MSBuild 17 doesn't support GetType()](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/calling-gettype-property-functions.md): MSBuild 17 no longer supports calling the GetType() instance method within property functions. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: C# code in templates not supported by earlier versions](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/csharp-template-code.md): Starting in .NET 6, the project templates that ship with the .NET SDK use the latest C# language features. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Option -p for 'dotnet run' deprecated](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/deprecate-p-option-dotnet-run.md): -p is deprecated as an abbreviation for --project, and using -p generates a warning. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Generate error for duplicate files in publish output](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/duplicate-files-in-output.md): The .NET SDK generates a new error (NETSDK1152) in cases where files from different source paths would be copied to the same file path… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: EditorConfig files implicitly included](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/editorconfig-additional-files.md): Roslyn analyzers added support for parsing and respecting .editorconfig file options before the compiler added support for these files. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: GetTargetFrameworkProperties and GetNearestTargetFramework removed](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/gettargetframeworkproperties-and-getnearesttargetframework-removed.md): The GetTargetFrameworkProperties target and GetNearestTargetFramework task have been removed from the .NET SDK. - [Breaking change: .NET can't be installed to custom location](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/install-location.md): You can no longer change the installation path of .NET with the Windows Installer package. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: `OutputType` no longer automatically set to WinExe](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/outputtype-not-set-automatically.md): In .NET 5, a change was made to automatically change OutputType from Exe to WinExe for WPF and Windows Forms apps. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Install location for x64 emulated on Arm64](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/path-x64-emulated.md): We've moved the install location of the x64 version of the .NET SDK and runtime on Arm64 hardware. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Publish ReadyToRun with --no-restore requires changes](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/publish-readytorun-requires-restore-change.md): If you publish a project with -p:PublishReadyToRun=true in addition to --no-restore, the project will only build with packages that were restored in a prior… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: runtimeconfig.dev.json file not generated](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/runtimeconfigdev-file.md): The .NET SDK no longer generates the runtimeconfig.dev.json file by default. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: `RuntimeIdentifier` warning if self-contained is unspecified](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/runtimeidentifier-self-contained.md): If you specify a RuntimeIdentifier in your project file or use the -r option with dotnet, the .NET SDK defaults the build, publish, and… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: .version file includes build version](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/version-file-entries.md): The .NET SDK installation folder (under dotnet/sdk/\<version>) contains a .version file. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Version requirements for .NET 6 SDK](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/vs-msbuild-version.md): Starting with the .NET SDK 6.0.300, the .NET SDK no longer loads in version 16.11 or earlier of Visual Studio or MSBuild. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Write reference assemblies to IntermediateOutputPath](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/6.0/write-reference-assemblies-to-obj.md): The .NET SDK now writes reference assemblies to the IntermediateOutputPath instead of the OutDir by default. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Automatic RuntimeIdentifier for publish only](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/7.0/automatic-rid-publish-only.md): In the 7.0.100 SDK, there was a change to automatically add <RuntimeIdentifier> to projects with properties that require a runtime identifier (RID). (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Automatic RuntimeIdentifier](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/7.0/automatic-runtimeidentifier.md): Projects that specify any of the following properties now get a runtime identifier (RID) automatically. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Serialization of custom types in .NET 7](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/7.0/custom-serialization.md): MSBuild in .NET 7 doesn't support serialization of custom BuildEventArgs-derived and ITaskItem-derived types via the BinaryFormatter serializer. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Tool manifests in root folder](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/7.0/manifest-search.md): .NET no longer looks for local tool manifest files in the root folder on Windows, unless overridden via the DOTNETTOOLSALLOWMANIFESTINROOT environment variable. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: ResolvePackageDependencies no longer called](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/7.0/resolvepackagedependencies.md): Previously, the .NET SDK called the ResolvePackageDependencies target in order to generate PackageDependencies and PackageDefinitions. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Side-by-side SDK installations](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/7.0/side-by-side-install.md): If a preview .NET 7 SDK is installed alongside the general availability (GA) version of the .NET 7 SDK, projects with workload dependencies such… (2 KB)

- [Breaking change: Version requirements for .NET 7 SDK](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/7.0/vs-msbuild-version.md): Certain .NET SDK versions require newer versions of Visual Studio and MSBuild. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Architecture option doesn't imply self-contained](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/arch-option.md): Up until now, the --arch option for dotnet CLI commands such as dotnet publish implied that the app was self-contained. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: SDK no longer changes console encoding after completion](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/console-encoding-fix.md): The bug mentioned in the CLI console output uses UTF-8 breaking change, where the .NET SDK changed the encoding of the entire console, has… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: CLI console output uses UTF-8](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/console-encoding.md): If the DOTNETCLIUILANGUAGE or VSLANG environment variable is set, the .NET CLI console output and input encoding changes to UTF-8, so that the code… (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: MSBuild custom derived build events deprecated](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/custombuildeventargs.md): Custom derived build events of any subclass of Microsoft.Build.Framework.BuildEventArgs by any build extensibility (mainly custom tasks) have been deprecated. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Setting DebugSymbols to false disables PDB generation](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/debugsymbols.md): The existing MSBuild documentation says that specifying DebugSymbols=false on the command line means that program database (.pdb) files aren't generated. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Containers default to use the 'latest' tag](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/default-image-tag.md): The default image tag used for .NET SDK-built containers changed from the value of the Version of the project to the value latest. (2 KB)

- [Breaking change: 'dotnet pack' uses Release configuration](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/dotnet-pack-config.md): The dotnet pack command, which packs code into a NuGet package, now uses the Release configuration instead of the Debug configuration by default. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'dotnet publish' uses Release configuration](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/dotnet-publish-config.md): The dotnet publish command now uses the Release configuration instead of the Debug configuration by default if the target framework is .NET 8 or… (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'dotnet restore' produces security vulnerability warnings](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/dotnet-restore-audit.md): The dotnet restore command, which restores the dependencies and tools of a project, now produces security vulnerability warnings by default. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Duplicate output for -getItem, -getProperty, and -getTargetResult](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/getx-duplicate-output.md): .NET SDK 8.0.200 introduced a regression in the new -getItem, -getProperty, and -getTargetResult MSBuild CLI options. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Implicit `using` for System.Net.Http no longer added](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/implicit-global-using-netfx.md): The implicit global using directive for the System.Net.Http namespace was removed for .NET Framework TFMs in an SDK-style project. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: MSBuild respects DOTNET_CLI_UI_LANGUAGE](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/msbuild-language.md): MSBuild now respects the DOTNETCLIUILANGUAGE environment variable and uses the language specified by DOTNETCLIUILANGUAGE for its command-line output. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: .NET SDK uses a smaller RID graph](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/rid-graph.md): Projects that target .NET 8 or later versions now use a smaller, "portable" runtime identifier (RID) graph. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Runtime-specific apps no longer self-contained](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/runtimespecific-app-default.md): Runtime-specific apps, or .NET apps with a RuntimeIdentifier, are no longer self-contained by default. (5 KB)
- [Breaking change: Source Link included in the .NET SDK](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/source-link.md): The Source Link build tooling is now included in the .NET SDK. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Trimming may not be used with .NET Standard or .NET Framework](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/trimming-unsupported-targetframework.md): Projects that set <PublishTrimmed>true</PublishTrimmed>, <IsTrimmable>true</IsTrimmable> or <EnableTrimAnalyzer>true</EnableTrimAnalyzer> with a TargetFramework that is any version of .NET Standard or .NET Framework produce a warning or… (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: Unlisted packages not installed by default for .NET tools](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/unlisted-versions.md): The dotnet tool install commands no longer install tools from unlisted versions of NuGet packages by default. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: .user file imported in outer builds](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/user-file.md): Previously, the SDK only imported .user configuration files in inner builds during a cross-targeted build. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Version requirements for .NET 8 SDK](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/8.0/version-requirements.md): Per the published support rules, the minimum Visual Studio and MSBuild version for each new major release is updated with a one quarter delay. (2 KB)
- [Default RID used when targeting .NET Framework](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/9.0/default-rid.md): In .NET 8, a change was introduced to use a smaller runtime identifier (RID) graph when targeting net8.0 and later versions. (2 KB)
- ['dotnet sln add' no longer allows invalid file names](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/9.0/dotnet-sln.md): Starting in .NET SDK 9.0.2xx, the dotnet sln add CLI command includes support for .slnx solution files using the vs-solutionpersistence serializer. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: 'dotnet watch' incompatible with Hot Reload for old frameworks](library/dotnet-core/compatibility/sdk/9.0/dotnet-watch.md): .NET 9 introduces a change that requires dotnet watch to launched with Hot Reload disabled for projects targeting .NET 5 or earlier versions. (1 KB)
